• DGFT – relaxed ‘One Star Export House’ eligibility condition, revising export performance criteria applicable across 3 
consecutive financial years 
• Now, export firms reporting required performance in any 2 of 3 preceding FYs will now be eligible to receive 
recognition 
• This relaxation does not apply to gems & jewellery sector 
- It already operates under a separate 2-year requirement under Foreign Trade Policy (FTP) 2023 
• Exporters holding a valid Importer-Exporter Code (IEC) can apply to DGFT for recognition as One Star, Two Star, Three 
Star, Four Star or Five Star Export House, based on their export performance 
- Certificates generally valid for 5 years from date of issue once granted 
• HQs of DGFT – New Delhi  |  Director General – Lav Aggarwal  |  Established in – 1991 
 
News - 5 
• Mizoram – currently has 79 monuments & archaeological sites declared as ‘State Protected Monuments’  
- under ‘Mizoram Ancient Monuments and Archaeological Sites and Remains Act, 2001’ 
• 2 sites in Champhai district – recognised as ‘Monuments of National Importance’ by ASI 
- Kawtchhuah Ropui & Ralven Puk at Vangchhia village  |  Lianpui Hmun (Lungphun Ropui) at Lianpui village 
• Mizoram Government – proposed 4 additional heritage sites for protected monument status, including Ruhro Puk in 
Saitual district 
• Vangchhia heritage site – ₹5.2 Cr allocated for infrastructure development  
• Mizoram: Capital – Aizawl  |  CM – Lalduhoma  |  Governor – General V. K. Singh 
 
News - 6 
• BIS expanded its Hallmark Unique Identification (HUID) -based silver hallmarking network to 341 centres across 102 
districts nationwide, with more than 25,000 registered silver jewellers now part of ecosystem 
• From Sep 1, 2025, BIS made HUID marking mandatory for any silver article that is hallmarked 
- Under revised IS 2112:2025 standard 
• HUID – a 6-digit alphanumeric code laser-marked on each hallmarked silver article 
- Alongside BIS Standard Mark, word “SILVER” & purity grade 
• Region leading the country in hallmarking volume – Southern Region (62%) 
- Followed by – Western Region (18%) & Central Region (10%) 
• HQs of BIS – Manek Bhawan, Old Delhi  |  Director General – Sanjay Garg

News - 11 
• Hindalco Industries – commissioned India’s first greenfield Superfine PPT ATH (Precipitated Aluminium  Trihydrate) 
manufacturing plant at Belagavi, Karnataka 
- It marks country’s first domestic production of this high-value specialty alumina material 
• Phase 1 facility has an annual production capacity of 30,000 tonnes 
• This plant is also world’s only specialty alumina facility to operate entirely on renewable energy 
• Superfine PPT ATH – a halogen-free flame-retardant material used in Halogen -Free Flame Retardant (HFFR) cables, 
polymer insulators, thermal insulation, composites & paints 
• HQs of Hindalco Industries – Mumbai, MH  |  Chairman – Kumar Mangalam Birla  |  Established in – 1958 
 
News - 12 
• NCCE (DGHS) – finalised India’s 1st uniform minimum national standards for mortuary & post-mortem infrastructure 
- Anchored in ‘Clinical Establishments (Registration and Regulation) Act, 2010’ 
- Applicable to public & private hospitals in States & UTs that have adopted this Act 
• Now, mortuaries are prohibited from being located in basements or near hospital waste disposal & dumping areas 
- They must be built on ground floor, with segregated entry points for body movement separate from general 
hospital patient traffic 
• Dead-body cold storage units must maintain a temperature range of 4°C to 8°C 
- Minimum storage capacity – four bodies 
- Each body must be assigned a unique serial number on its storage cabin for traceability 
- Medico-legal case bodies must be stored separately from non-medico-legal remains 
• It also prescribes a dedicated autopsy block of about 4,000 square feet 
 
News - 13 
• Dr. Jitendra Singh – launched 5th edition of “Swachh Sagar, Surakshit Sagar 2026” (SSSS v5.0) in New Delhi 
- It is a week-long nationwide coastal cleanliness & ocean conservation campaign 
- Led by – Ministry of Earth Sciences (MoES)  |  It will run from Sep 12 to 19, 2026 
- Organised under “Jan Bhagidari” (people’s participation) framework 
• Govt unveiled ‘SSSS 5.0’ website, ocean conservation pledge, an introductory video & an activity schedule 
- Alongside ‘SAGAR PRAHARI’ app for citizen participation 
• Swachh Sagar, Surakshit Sagar – originally launched by MoES in 2022 as a 75-day citizen-led coastal clean-up campaign 
- It has been described as world’s 1st & longest-running coastal cleanup campaign of its kind 
• MoS (I/C) in Earth Sciences: Dr. Jitendra Singh  |  Constituency – Udhampur, J&K

• TRAI – issued ‘Standards of Quality of Service (Duration of Advertisements in Television Channels) (Repealing) 
Regulations, 2026’ 
- It repeals – ‘Standards of Quality of Service (Duration of Advertisements in Television Channels) 
Regulations, 2012’  
• 2012 regulations – had prescribed a ceiling of 12 minutes of advertisements in a clock hour of television programming

• RBI – released draft ‘Reserve Bank of India (Know Your Customer) Amendment Directions, 2026’ for public comments 
- It inserts a Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) for banks to handle accounts & transactions suspected of money-
mule activity or cyber-enabled financial fraud 
• For AI/ML-flagged unusual transfers of ₹1,000 or more, banks to place a temporary debit hold limited to the disputed 
transaction amount, freezing the entire account 
• Account holders – 20 calendar days to explain or submit documents  |  Banks – 10 calendar days to assess the response 
• Temporary debit holds – generally not to exceed 60 days  |  Law -enforcement agencies – 30 days from referral to 
issue a statutory restraint order 
• Applicable to – Commercial Banks (including SFBs, PBs, RRBs & Local Area Banks) & Urban Cooperative Banks 
- Effective from – Apr 1, 2027 (banks may adopt earlier) 
• Per TransUnion report (Jun 2026) – nearly 7.1% of attempted digital consumer transactions in India suspected of fraud 
in 2025  (almost double the global rate of 3.8%)

• ‘Pradhan Mantri Kisan Maandhan Yojana (PM-KMY)’ – marked 7 years of implementation

• PM-KMY – a voluntary, contributory old-age pension scheme for Small & Marginal Farmers (SMFs) 
- It is a Central Sector Scheme administered by Department of Agriculture & Farmers Welfare (MoA&FW) 
• Eligibility – landholding SMFs (both male & female) owning up to 2 hectares of cultivable land

• On a beneficiary’s death – spouse entitled to 50% of pension as family pension 
• Farmers can also use their PM-KISAN benefits for voluntary contributions towards PM-KMY 
• Highest enrolment – Haryana (~5.75 lakh)  |  Followed by – Bihar (3.46 lakh+) 
- Jharkhand & Uttar Pradesh – over 2.5 lakh each  |  Chhattisgarh – more than 2 lakh

• Tribunals Reforms Bill, 2026 – provides for “National Tribunals Data Grid”, a centralised digital repository covering 
case-related information of 16 tribunals & appellate bodies listed in First Schedule, including Income Tax Appellate 
Tribunal & National Green Tribunal 
• Bill repeals ‘Tribunals Reforms Act, 2021’ 
• National Tribunals Commission (NTC) – responsible for developing & maintaining National Tribunals Data Grid 
- It comprises = Chairperson + 2 Judicial Members + 2 Technical Members  
- Chairperson must be former Supreme Court Judge or Chief Justice of High Court 
- Members are appointed by Central Govt for 5 years or till age 70, whichever is earlier 
- Technical Members require minimum 25 years’ experience in public administration, finance, law, accountancy, 
banking, management or technology 
• Tribunal pendency – 5.24 lakh+ cases pending across various tribunals 
 
News - 401 
• DoCA (MoCAF&PD) – notified “Legal Metrology (Indian Standard Time) Rules, 2026” 
- Framed under Section 52 of Legal Metrology Act, 2009 
• Rules establish Indian Standard Time (IST) as single, uniform legal reference for legal, administrative, commercial & 
official purposes across India 
- It will come into force 180 days after publication in Official Gazette 
• Govt offices & public institutions must synchronise their clocks using Network Time Protocol & Precision Time Protocol 
• IST – maintained by CSIR-National Physical Laboratory (CSIR-NPL) & not by India Meteorological Department or ISRO 
- It is calculated as Coordinated Universal Time (UTC) plus 5 hours 30 minutes, based on 82.5°E longitude reference

• DoCA (MoCAFPD) – notified “Consumer Protection (E-Commerce) (Amendment) Rules, 2026”, w.e.f. Jan 1, 2027 
- Under Section 101(2)(zg) of Consumer Protection Act, 2019 
- It amends – Consumer Protection (E-Commerce) Rules, 2020 
• Amended rules redefine “ranking” to cover relative prominence or relevance given to sellers, goods or services on an 
e-commerce platform, irrespective of technological means used 
• Where a price reduction is announced, platforms must display both reduced & prior price 
• “Prior price” – lowest price offered in preceding 30 days

• They must conduct an annual self-audit & prominently display a compliance certificate 
• Grievance officers must acknowledge consumer complaints within 48 hours, provide a copy of recorded complaint 
- They must redress complaints within 1 month of receipt 
• Every e-commerce entity must become a partner in convergence process of National Consumer Helpline (NCH)

• Amendment was notified by – MHA, in exercise of powers conferred under Article 263 of Constitution 
• Existing proviso in Clause 2 of 1990 Order – substituted with a new provision specifying 4 situations in which concerned 
Governor or Lieutenant Governor shall be invited to attend Council meetings 
- When a proclamation under Article 356 is in force in a state, its Governor shall be invited 
- When an order under Article 239AB is in force for NCT of Delhi, its Lieutenant Governor shall be invited 
- When an order under Section 51 of ‘Government of Union Territories Act, 1963’ is in force, Lieutenant Governor 
of Puducherry shall be invited

• Tata Consultancy Services (TCS) has announced a definitive agreement to acquire 100% of Porsche AG’s IT and 
management consulting subsidiary, MHP Management- und IT-Beratung GmbH (MHP), for an enterprise value of 
€320 million (approx. ₹3,573 crore). Alongside this acquisition, TCS and Porsche have entered a massive 5 -year 
strategic partnership valued at €1.25 billion (approx. $1.5 billion), anchoring Porsche as a major client for the Indian 
IT giant. 
 
• ICICI Bank has completed the acquisition of an additional 2% equity stake in its subsidiary, ICICI Prudential Life 
Insurance Company, for an aggregate consideration of ₹1,470 crore. 
 
• Udaan has acquired LYNK Logistics Limited (LYNK), Swiggy’s wholly -owned, technology-driven retail distribution 
platform, for ₹500 crore. The transaction will be settled through the issuance of preference equity shares to Swiggy 
in Trustroot Internet Privat e Limited (TIPL), the parent entity of Udaan. As part of the transaction, Swiggy will 
acquire about 2.8% stake in Udaan and make a primary equity investment of ₹75 crore in TIPL for an additional 
0.4% stake in Udaan. 
 
• Novartis India has acquired the Minipress and Minipres trademarks in India and certain related intellectual property 
rights from Pfizer Inc and Pfizer Products Inc for Rs 1,250 crore. 
 
• RBI approved Life Insurance Corporation of India (LIC) to acquire an aggregate holding of up to 9.99% of the paid -
up share capital or voting rights in ICICI Bank. 
 
• ICICI Prudential Asset Management Company has received approval from RBI to acquire aggregate holding of up to 
9.95% in four banks - CSB Bank, DCB Bank, Kotak Mahindra Bank and AU Small Finance Bank. 
 
• CCI officially approved the acquisition of 3 prominent cricket franchises under the “Royals” umbrella. The $1.65 
billion (~₹15,600 crore) deal involves the takeover of the Rajasthan Royals (Indian Premier League), the Paarl Royals 
(South Africa’s SA20), an d the Barbados Royals (Caribbean Premier League). The teams are being acquired by 
Westview Cricket Limited (a UK -registered investment vehicle owned by the billionaire Lakshmi Mittal family), 
alongside Poonawalla Sports and Fitness Private Limited, which is wholly owned by Adar Poonawalla. 
 
• Jammu and Kashmir (J&K) Bank signed an independent escrow agreement to sell its 0.5% stake in PNB MetLife India 
Insurance Company Ltd to MetLife International Holdings, LLC for ₹120.10 crore. 
 
• Mastercard has officially exited Indian merchant commerce platform Pine Labs by offloading its entire 4.31% stake 
for ₹933.57 crore through a series of open-market block deals on the BSE.
